# Seed 75 on the AMD Ryzen 7 PRO 8840HS - the stopping count's second run: the fastest gains past its threshold, the hypervolume does not (Iteration 553)

`seed 75 10h then experiment review-checks.py spread then experiment appbench.py --zip ~/Downloads/appbench.zip --sample 16 then experiment clockcheck.py --sample 8 then experiment truefronts.py` at 8f2c3afa. The chain: done. 35,161 designs, generations 0-137: 22,713 ok, 7,981 stopped after one round, 4,430 screened, 37 wrong output: apps; 3,516 designs an hour.

**The front**: the true one 266 designs - the report's 266, the same.

**The fixed-box hypervolume**: true front 266 (266 inside the box), hypervolume 0.34851 | fastest 0.1363, smallest 14095, least memory 112 KB, fastest at 32 bits 0.1723. Against the method's one earlier run, seed 74's 0.35667: 2.3% below - no gain by the hypervolume (a gain is 1% above).

**The extremes**: fastest 4c9c2d9075 0.1363, smallest b2eb2ef4a9 14,095 bytes, least memory 112 KB, fastest at 32 bits 4c9c2d9075 0.1723.

**One round first**: 7,981 children stopped after one round, 26.1% of those measured; 812 audited in full, 0 of them on the front.

**The operators**: borrowed 39.2%, crossover 26.7%, mutation 34.1% of the children; the last fifth borrowed 39.7%, crossover 22.9%, mutation 37.4%; the front's entries per child 0.759% (266 of 35,029).

**The JIT designs**: 94 of the front, 26 of its fastest tenth's 26; jitmul 55, jitfuse 19, jitexec 1, jitos 43, jitrp 24.

**The fast end re-measured** (remeasure.json, 112 designs): the fastest ten 1.053 re-measured / in-run, the JIT designs 1.026, the rest 1.007.

**The experiments**: the spread at generation 102 (session 62) - seed 75 ahead at the 64-bit fast end, 0.1384 against seeds 71-74's 0.1452-0.1520; at 32 bits 0.1783 (seed 74's 0.1731); smallest 14,103 (73: 14,079); appbench - **Spearman, in-run speed against the applications' geometric mean: 0.835 over 16 designs.**; the clock test - **Medians** - time against the run: alone 1.084, beside benchmarks 1.075, beside compiles 1.079; time against alone: benchmarks 0.989, compiles 0.984; cycles against alone: benchmarks 1.001, compiles 1.001.; truefronts - 23 runs.

**The remote checks** (results/xcheck-seed75.md): dream.lan-prebuilt 24 designs measured on both: Spearman 0.910; dream.lan-prebuilt32 30 designs measured on both: Spearman 0.906; dream.lan-prebuilt64 30 designs measured on both: Spearman 0.945; rage.lan-native 8 designs measured on both: Spearman 0.762.

**The stopping rule (GOALS.md) - seed 75 gains, by the speed extreme alone.** Against seed 74, the method's one earlier
run: the fastest 4c9c2d9075 0.1363 against 3cf671fa08 0.1402 - 2.8% faster, past the 2% threshold (0.1374); its re-
measure at the run's end, 0.1371, is past it too, narrowly. The hypervolume 2.3% below; the smallest 14,095 against
14,079, the least memory 112 KB both, the fastest at 32 bits 0.1723 against 0.1740 (1.0%) - none past its threshold. One
design decides it (generation 129, d256=1 and hotinl=10 on a JIT design); the speed's gains of 2% or more stopped at
generation 49, the rest of the way in smaller steps. The count: seed 74 the first, seed 75 a gain - so seed 76 cannot
end it; the earliest stop is after seed 77, if seeds 76 and 77 both gain nothing against the best of the method's runs
(hypervolume 0.36024, fastest 0.1336, smallest 13,938, memory 108 KB, 32-bit 0.1688).

**Prediction 549 fails** - "no extreme past its threshold": the fastest is, by 0.8 points; its hypervolume half holds.

**The votes** (the true front): all four this run - rtimm, abortif, jitdoes, jitcfa, jitrp, jitret, factor. Over 73-75
**three freeze candidates, 12 of 12 with no keep vote: jitrp** (since 74), **jitdoes and jitret** (new - jitret's keep
vote of seed 72 has left the window). A freeze changes the method and restarts the stopping count - the owner's to
decide; none frozen. jitos, without a vote since 534, is back on 43 of the front (seed 74: none).

**The rules** (review-checks.py rules): 1 the verdict - the stopping rule's, above (no comparison step in the chain); 2
ATTENTION - jitret a candidate now; 3 PASS (1.047; seeds 73-74 ATTENTION at 1.070 and 1.066); 4 PASS; 5 PASS (41 32-bit
failures, every one cpt16's reach - expected, now named); 6 PASS (14,095); 7 PASS (rtiplus on 19%, working); 8 tail
retired - its votes count nothing; 9 PROPOSE (no design relies on the rtimm=2 / rtfuse overlap: rtfuse to govern - the
owner's); 10 run-tests and the corpus pass (the chain on master, Iteration 553).

**The clock test, with cycles** - the perf hold worked: the cycle columns filled at 02:15, eight hours into an
unattended chain, and perf-hold.py logged perf_event_paranoid 4 again after the measurement (runs/perf-hold.log stays on
the laptop). The fastest eight's time against the run: 1.084 alone, 1.075 beside benchmarks, 1.079 beside compiles. The
user-space cycle ratio did not move between the conditions (1.001, 1.001) while the time ratio moved 1.1-1.6%: what
varies is outside user space. Not the clock - the cycles agree with results/drafts/fast-end-offset-550.md. A lead for
the offset, not a finding: the cycle ratio sits well below the time ratio (4c9c2d9075: 0.116 against 0.147); a cost
outside user space that does not shrink with a design's own work - about 3.5% of s6's time here - weighs seven times
more on a design at 0.136 than on one at s6's speed, the pattern the curse shows (the fastest tenth 1.047, the rest
1.007). The clock test could split user and system time (rusage) to test it.

**Held out** (heldout.py): sieve's Spearman 0.963, the fastest third 0.845, the fastest tenth 0.781; regret +4.3% - the
selection's fastest 4c9c2d9075 0.072 on sieve, the held-out fastest b502af976b 0.069 (seed 74: +0.0%).

**The curse** (re-measured / in-run): the fastest tenth 1.047, the fastest third 1.021 - the in-chain re-measure's
offset smaller than seeds 73-74's (1.070, 1.066); the clock test about 40 minutes later, 1.084.

**Progress**: the smallest gained in 15 generations, the last at 136 of 137 (24 bytes in the last third); the best speed
by 2% or more in 13, the last at 49.

Facts: the pack's own checks PASS, 7 IDENTICAL, jail PASS; results-measured-here.patch 4 files, not applied; fail32 - 41
failures at 32 bits, every one expected (cpt16's reach); the 32-bit speeds against the laptop's at dream 0.937 (24
designs), the Tegra 0.800 (4); generic - c508a88db0 the one design measured on all three machine classes (0.428).

---

# Evolved VM designs

30731 designs evaluated, 22713 alive, 4430 screened - not measured (Iteration 200). Speed is the geometric mean of median cpu time / hand-made s6, long workloads (Iteration 100) over kernel, fib, corpus, loop, bubble, ackermann, apps,
relative to s6-cv8b; size is the self-hosting image, binary the stripped engine. sieve, parse is held out.
The front is over three objectives, equal (Iteration 75): speed, binary + image, peak memory - memory within 8 KB a tie (Iteration 77).


The Q-best design (binary + image x run time x memory - the knee; information): 74282f72b1, speed 0.372, binary + image 21,573, memory 124 KB - Q 0.302 of s6-cv8b's.

## The front's genomes

Each design above, and any other the front was measured again with, as one JSON line: its record without the raw timings. A tool that takes a database (--db) reads this report as one - the lines that are not JSON are skipped - so results/ is all a new chat needs to rebuild a front design.
